#ACT

; XYDP-BEGIN xy.optional.recycle.configurable SHA256=3f122dcea6f904462359057666caf497935b2593d2ccb08d3582a77b369051a6
; 表格驱动装备回收；分类映射注释用于升级时保留人物勾选状态。
; XY-RECYCLE-CATEGORY A FLAG=11 STATUS=ACTIVE
; XY-RECYCLE-CATEGORY B FLAG=12 STATUS=RETIRED
; XY-RECYCLE-CATEGORY C FLAG=13 STATUS=RETIRED

[@UserCmd31]
#IF
#ACT
GOTO @XY_RECYCLE_PANEL_MAIN
BREAK

[@XY_RECYCLE_COMMAND]
#IF
#ACT
GOTO @XY_RECYCLE_PANEL_MAIN
BREAK

[@XY_RECYCLE_PANEL_MAIN]
#IF
#ACT
GOTO @XY_RECYCLE_P01_ROUTE_ROOT
BREAK

[@XY_RECYCLE_P01_ROUTE_ROOT]
#IF
CHECK [11] 0
#ACT
GOTO @XY_RECYCLE_P01_ROUTE_0
BREAK
#ELSEACT
GOTO @XY_RECYCLE_P01_ROUTE_1
BREAK

[@XY_RECYCLE_P01_ROUTE_0]
#IF
#ACT
GOTO @XY_RECYCLE_P01_STATE_0
BREAK

[@XY_RECYCLE_P01_ROUTE_1]
#IF
#ACT
GOTO @XY_RECYCLE_P01_STATE_1
BREAK

[@XY_RECYCLE_P01_STATE_0]
#SAY
<装备自动回收（空余少于40格触发）/SCOLOR=250>\
<第1/1页；勾选√即纳入自动回收。/SCOLOR=146>\
\
<制式装备/SCOLOR=230> <□/@XY_RECYCLE_TOGGLE_001>\
\
<一键回收已勾选装备/@XY_RECYCLE_PANEL_APPLY>  <全部取消/@XY_RECYCLE_AUTO_DISABLE>  <关闭/@EXIT>\

[@XY_RECYCLE_P01_STATE_1]
#SAY
<装备自动回收（空余少于40格触发）/SCOLOR=250>\
<第1/1页；勾选√即纳入自动回收。/SCOLOR=146>\
\
<制式装备/SCOLOR=230> <√/@XY_RECYCLE_TOGGLE_001>\
\
<一键回收已勾选装备/@XY_RECYCLE_PANEL_APPLY>  <全部取消/@XY_RECYCLE_AUTO_DISABLE>  <关闭/@EXIT>\

[@XY_RECYCLE_TOGGLE_001]
#IF
CHECK [11] 1
#ACT
SET [11] 0
SetOnTimer 20 1
GOTO @XY_RECYCLE_P01_ROUTE_ROOT
BREAK
#ELSEACT
SET [11] 1
SetOnTimer 20 1
GOTO @XY_RECYCLE_P01_ROUTE_ROOT
BREAK

[@XY_RECYCLE_PANEL_APPLY]
#IF
CHECK [11] 0
#ACT
MESSAGEBOX 请先勾选至少一个回收分类。
BREAK
#ELSEACT
GOTO @XY_RECYCLE_SELECTED_APPLY
BREAK

[@XY_RECYCLE_AUTO_COMMAND]
#IF
#ACT
GOTO @XY_RECYCLE_PANEL_MAIN
BREAK

[@XY_RECYCLE_AUTO_ENABLE]
#IF
CHECK [11] 0
#ACT
MESSAGEBOX 请先在装备回收页面勾选需要自动回收的分类。
BREAK
#ELSEACT
SetOnTimer 20 1
GOTO @XY_RECYCLE_PANEL_MAIN
BREAK

[@XY_RECYCLE_AUTO_DISABLE]
#IF
#ACT
SET [11] 0
SetOffTimer 20
GOTO @XY_RECYCLE_PANEL_MAIN
BREAK

[@XY_RECYCLE_SELECTED_APPLY]
#IF
#ACT
MOV N$XY_RecycleEquipBonus 0
; XY_EQUIP_MAKER_RECYCLE_BONUS_QFUNCTION_ANCHOR
; XYDP-BEGIN xy.event.gostoc-title recycle-qfunction SHA256=6d5d2608afce3b996542fae4513290f7ee9ef6c9e367e546906b040908746748
#IF
CHECKFENGHAO 小偷葛托克
#ACT
INC N$XY_RecycleEquipBonus 66
; XYDP-END xy.event.gostoc-title recycle-qfunction
; XY-EQUIP-MAKER 回收刀: 回收增加+200%
#IF
CHECKITEMW 回收刀 1
#ACT
INC N$XY_RecycleEquipBonus 200
; 分类A => 褪色旅人戒指
#IF
#ACT
MOV N$XY_RecycleBatchBefore 0
MOV N$XY_RecycleBatchAfter 0
MOV N$XY_RecycleBatchRemoved 0
#IF
CHECK [11] 1
CHECKITEM 褪色旅人戒指 1
#ACT
GetItemCount 0 褪色旅人戒指 N$XY_RecycleBatchBefore
TAKE 褪色旅人戒指 <$STR(N$XY_RecycleBatchBefore)>
GetItemCount 0 褪色旅人戒指 N$XY_RecycleBatchAfter
FORMULATION <$STR(N$XY_RecycleBatchBefore)>-<$STR(N$XY_RecycleBatchAfter)> N$XY_RecycleBatchRemoved
#IF
CHECK [11] 1
LARGE N$XY_RecycleBatchRemoved 0
#ACT
MOV N$XY_RecycleBatchReward 0
MOV N$XY_RecycleBatchBonusReward 0
FORMULATION <$STR(N$XY_RecycleBatchRemoved)>*100 N$XY_RecycleBatchReward
FORMULATION (<$STR(N$XY_RecycleBatchReward)>*<$STR(N$XY_RecycleEquipBonus)>)/100 N$XY_RecycleBatchBonusReward
INC N$XY_RecycleBatchReward <$STR(N$XY_RecycleBatchBonusReward)>
GOLDCOUNT + <$STR(N$XY_RecycleBatchReward)>
SENDMSG 6 回收：褪色旅人戒指×<$STR(N$XY_RecycleBatchRemoved)>，获得金币<$STR(N$XY_RecycleBatchReward)>（装备回收增加<$STR(N$XY_RecycleEquipBonus)>%）。
MOV N$XY_RecycleBatchReward 0
MOV N$XY_RecycleBatchBonusReward 0
FORMULATION <$STR(N$XY_RecycleBatchRemoved)>*1 N$XY_RecycleBatchReward
FORMULATION (<$STR(N$XY_RecycleBatchReward)>*<$STR(N$XY_RecycleEquipBonus)>)/100 N$XY_RecycleBatchBonusReward

; XYDP-BEGIN xy.optional.recycle.drop-filter SHA256=5670ba6b5eb35204f20665a1c1abef5c7bdf1c2760bffbb454c293a315c33e83
[@XY_RECYCLE_DROP_FILTER_COMMAND]
#IF
EQUAL N$XY_RecycleDropFilterEnabled 0
#ACT
GOTO @XY_RECYCLE_DROP_FILTER_ENABLE
BREAK
#ELSEACT
GOTO @XY_RECYCLE_DROP_FILTER_DISABLE
BREAK

[@XY_RECYCLE_DROP_FILTER_ENABLE]
#IF
#ACT
FILTERGLOBALMSG 1 1
MOV N$XY_RecycleDropFilterEnabled 1
SENDMSG 6 全服掉落提示信息已屏蔽。
BREAK

[@XY_RECYCLE_DROP_FILTER_DISABLE]
#IF
#ACT
FILTERGLOBALMSG 1 0
MOV N$XY_RecycleDropFilterEnabled 0
SENDMSG 6 全服掉落提示信息已恢复。
BREAK
; XYDP-END xy.optional.recycle.drop-filter

<退出/@EXIT>
; XYDP-END xy.optional.bag.function-service

MOV N$XY_MDA_Final <$STR(N$XY_MDA_Raw)>

#IF
EQUAL N$XY_MDA_Final 0
#ACT
StruckDamageAbsorb * 100 1 1
MOV N$XY_MDA_TimerActive 0
BREAK
#IF
LARGE N$XY_MDA_Final 0
#ACT
StruckDamageAbsorb * 100 <$STR(N$XY_MDA_Final)> 300

#IF
EQUAL N$XY_MDA_TimerLoop 0

DELAYGOTO 240 @XYDP_MonsterAbsorbTick
BREAK
#IF
EQUAL N$XY_MDA_TimerActive 0
#ACT
MOV N$XY_MDA_TimerLoop 0
BREAK

; XYDP-LAB-BEGIN xy.lab.execution.monster-map0
; Standalone map-rule pilot. Do not merge into production before acceptance.
[@XYDP_ExecutionLoadMapRule]
#IF
#ACT
MOV N$XY_EXEC_MONSTER_MapEnabled 0
MOV N$XY_EXEC_MONSTER_MapRequiredToughness 0
MOV N$XY_EXEC_MONSTER_MapChanceBP 0
MOV N$XY_EXEC_MONSTER_MapPVEToughness 0
; XY_EXECUTION_MONSTER_MAP_RULES_BEGIN
#IF
ISONMAP 0
#ACT
MOV N$XY_EXEC_MONSTER_MapEnabled 1
MOV N$XY_EXEC_MONSTER_MapRequiredToughness 100
MOV N$XY_EXEC_MONSTER_MapChanceBP 1000
MOV N$XY_EXEC_MONSTER_MapPVEToughness 60
; XY_EXECUTION_MONSTER_MAP_RULES_END
MOV N$XY_EXEC_MONSTER_MapRuleLoaded 1

[@StruckDamage]
; XYDP-HOOK-BEGIN xy.lab.execution.monster-map0 StruckDamage
#IF
EQUAL N$XY_EXEC_MONSTER_MapEnabled 1
NOT CHECKCURRTARGETRACE = 0
EQUAL N$XY_EXEC_MONSTER_ACTIVE 1
#ACT
ChangeDamageValue 1 + <$STR(N$XY_EXEC_MONSTER_BonusPercent)>
BREAK
#IF
EQUAL N$XY_EXEC_MONSTER_MapEnabled 1
NOT CHECKCURRTARGETRACE = 0
EQUAL N$XY_EXEC_MONSTER_ACTIVE 0
LARGE N$XY_EXEC_MONSTER_MapRequiredToughness 0
#ACT
MOV N$XY_EXEC_MONSTER_ChanceBP <$STR(N$XY_EXEC_MONSTER_MapChanceBP)>
FORMULATION <$STR(N$XY_EXEC_MONSTER_MapChanceBP)>-((<$STR(N$XY_EXEC_MONSTER_MapChanceBP)>*<$STR(N$XY_EXEC_Toughness)>)/<$STR(N$XY_EXEC_MONSTER_MapRequiredToughness)>) N$XY_EXEC_MONSTER_EffectiveBP
#IF
EQUAL N$XY_EXEC_MONSTER_MapEnabled 1
NOT CHECKCURRTARGETRACE = 0
EQUAL N$XY_EXEC_MONSTER_ACTIVE 0
LARGE N$XY_EXEC_MONSTER_EffectiveBP 0
RANDOMEX <$STR(N$XY_EXEC_MONSTER_EffectiveBP)> 10000
#ACT
MOV N$XY_EXEC_MONSTER_Triggered 1
#IF
EQUAL N$XY_EXEC_MONSTER_MapEnabled 1
NOT CHECKCURRTARGETRACE = 0
EQUAL N$XY_EXEC_MONSTER_Triggered 1
#ACT
FORMULATION <$STR(N$XY_EXEC_MONSTER_EffectiveBP)>/100 N$XY_EXEC_MONSTER_Tier
#IF
EQUAL N$XY_EXEC_MONSTER_MapEnabled 1
NOT CHECKCURRTARGETRACE = 0
EQUAL N$XY_EXEC_MONSTER_Triggered 1
SMALL N$XY_EXEC_MONSTER_Tier 1
#ACT
MOV N$XY_EXEC_MONSTER_Tier 1
#IF
EQUAL N$XY_EXEC_MONSTER_MapEnabled 1
NOT CHECKCURRTARGETRACE = 0
EQUAL N$XY_EXEC_MONSTER_Triggered 1
LARGE N$XY_EXEC_MONSTER_Tier 100
#ACT
MOV N$XY_EXEC_MONSTER_Tier 100
#IF
EQUAL N$XY_EXEC_MONSTER_MapEnabled 1
NOT CHECKCURRTARGETRACE = 0
EQUAL N$XY_EXEC_MONSTER_Triggered 1
LARGE N$XY_EXEC_MONSTER_Tier 0
#ACT
FORMULATION 100+(<$STR(N$XY_EXEC_MONSTER_Tier)>-1)*80/3 N$XY_EXEC_MONSTER_BonusPercent
FORMULATION (5+(<$STR(N$XY_EXEC_MONSTER_Tier)>-1))*200 N$XY_EXEC_MONSTER_DurationMs
#IF
EQUAL N$XY_EXEC_MONSTER_MapEnabled 1
NOT CHECKCURRTARGETRACE = 0
EQUAL N$XY_EXEC_MONSTER_Triggered 1
LARGE N$XY_EXEC_MONSTER_DurationMs 0
#ACT
MOV N$XY_EXEC_MONSTER_ACTIVE 1
ChangeDamageValue 1 + <$STR(N$XY_EXEC_MONSTER_BonusPercent)>
ChangeSpeed 1 -10
#CALL [\玄渊实验室\处决\处决重甲跪地外观.txt] @XY_EXEC_VISUAL_APPLY
FORMULATION <$STR(N$XY_EXEC_MONSTER_DurationMs)>/1000 N$XY_EXEC_MONSTER_DurationWholeSec
FORMULATION (<$STR(N$XY_EXEC_MONSTER_DurationMs)>-(<$STR(N$XY_EXEC_MONSTER_DurationWholeSec)>*1000))/100 N$XY_EXEC_MONSTER_DurationTenth
SendNewLineMsg 1 251 0 16 120 5 0 您已被击倒！||移动速度降低50%，持续<$STR(N$XY_EXEC_MONSTER_DurationWholeSec)>.<$STR(N$XY_EXEC_MONSTER_DurationTenth)>秒||持续期间受到<$STR(N$XY_EXEC_MONSTER_BonusPercent)>%额外伤害。
MOV N$XY_EXEC_MONSTER_SlowActive 1
DelayCall <$STR(N$XY_EXEC_MONSTER_DurationMs)> @XYDP_ExecutionMonsterClear
SENDMSG 6 MONSTER_EXECUTION_MAP_RULE_TRIGGERED
MOV N$XY_EXEC_MONSTER_Triggered 0
BREAK
; XYDP-HOOK-END xy.lab.execution.monster-map0 StruckDamage

[@XYDP_ExecutionMonsterClear]
#IF
EQUAL N$XY_EXEC_MONSTER_SlowActive 1
#ACT
#CALL [\玄渊实验室\处决\处决重甲跪地外观.txt] @XY_EXEC_VISUAL_CLEAR
ChangeSpeed 1 0
MOV N$XY_EXEC_MONSTER_SlowActive 0
MOV N$XY_EXEC_MONSTER_ACTIVE 0
MOV N$XY_EXEC_MONSTER_BonusPercent 0
MOV N$XY_EXEC_MONSTER_DurationMs 0
MOV N$XY_EXEC_MONSTER_EffectiveBP 0
MOV N$XY_EXEC_MONSTER_Tier 0
BREAK
#IF
#ACT
MOV N$XY_EXEC_MONSTER_ACTIVE 0
MOV N$XY_EXEC_MONSTER_BonusPercent 0
MOV N$XY_EXEC_MONSTER_DurationMs 0
MOV N$XY_EXEC_MONSTER_EffectiveBP 0
MOV N$XY_EXEC_MONSTER_Tier 0

[@EnterMap]
; XYDP-HOOK-BEGIN xy.lab.execution.monster-map0 EnterMap
#IF
#ACT
MOV N$XY_EXEC_MONSTER_MapEnabled 0
MOV N$XY_EXEC_MONSTER_MapPVEToughness 0
MOV N$XY_EXEC_PVEMapEffectiveBP 0
MOV N$XY_EXEC_MONSTER_MapRuleLoaded 0
DELAYGOTO 1 @XYDP_ExecutionLoadMapRule
; XYDP-HOOK-END xy.lab.execution.monster-map0 EnterMap
#IF
#ACT
BREAK
; XYDP-LAB-END xy.lab.execution.monster-map0

SET [400] 1
#IF

CHECK [400] 1
CHECK [401] 1
CHECK [402] 1
#ACT
SET [600] 1
SENDMSG 6 [装备收集] 集齐漂流群岛，伤害系数提升1%。
SENDMSG 6 [装备收集] 木剑点亮成功，获得：神力倍攻%+1。
DELAYGOTO 1 @XYDP_RecalcPower
DELAYGOTO 1 @XYDP_RecalcBlast
DELAYGOTO 1 @XYDP_RecalcDrop
DELAYGOTO 1 @XYDP_RecalcMonsterAbsorb
DELAYGOTO 1 @XYDP_RecalcSustain
DELAYGOTO 1 @XYDP_ShowAttrBuff
GOTO @XY_COLLECTION_CAT_001_PAGE_01
BREAK
